Maximum is across conditions within one kernel; selection is ascending across kernels. Preserve parameter-level J_params=max_c(mean_f L_fc) unchanged, do not average condition Losses or sample-pool them for this kernel comparator, and do not refit or rescore on other folds. Use the explanatory term worst-condition Loss to distinguish aggregation from the lower-is-better selection direction. |

| REQ-124 | For final fold-kernel selection within each retained best parameter configuration, use decimal half-up rounding of the final worst-condition Loss to four decimal places. Among Validation-passing fold kernels, select the two lowest rounded values and every additional kernel tied with the second-ranked rounded value. The retained count may exceed two; do not break these ties by fold index, History, alpha, K, raw sub-rounding differences or completion order. Round only the final max-condition scalar for this final selection; retain raw per-condition MSEs, raw Validation gate checks, parameter coarse/fine ranking and convergence, and original evidence values. Preserve independent per-configuration pools, exact kernel/calibration identity, no final refit and fixed worker ceilings. Fewer-than-two passing kernels remain pending. |

| REQ-125 | After Validation filtering within each retained best parameter configuration, use the actual passing fold-kernel count: with at least two, apply REQ-124 two-plus-second-place-ties; with exactly one, let that unchanged kernel proceed to Score/threshold/Qualification; with zero, send no kernel from that configuration onward while other retained best configurations continue. If every retained best configuration has zero passing kernels, record Validation failed and advance the existing unsuccessful Speed/Offset fallback, preserving eligible prior qualified results. Do not fill slots with rejected kernels or higher-Loss parameter configurations, relax the Validation allowance, resample the same round, lower N or create an extra final fit. This is a Validation admission outcome, not missing Cycle counts, invalid numerical evidence or an infrastructure failure; retain those separate policies. |

| REQ-126 | Within each active Speed x Offset condition, balance the number of whole valid Cycles across the N held-out folds: each fold receives floor(n/N) or ceil(n/N) Cycles, so counts differ by at most one. Preserve every Cycle assigned to Validation exactly once, at least one Validation Cycle per condition per fold and at least one remaining Training Cycle from each recording in every fold. Do not split Cycles or balance by eligible sample counts instead; variable Cycle lengths can yield unequal sample counts despite balanced Cycle counts. Preflight validates the actual constrained assignment and preserves terminal allocation-failure handling. Which Cycle identities receive which folds, run-seed mechanics and saved assignment provenance remain to reconcile. |

| REQ-127 | Automatically generate a fresh sampling seed for every Start Learning and capture it once for that run; no manual seed entry is required. Use reproducible round-specific sampling derived from that run seed while obeying REQ-126 and all allocation constraints. Record the actual per-round Cycle-to-held-out-fold membership with recording/Cycle/source identities, active scope, N and relevant sampler/settings provenance. All candidate jobs in the same round share that captured plan. Persist the seed and actual maps with associated results through explicit project Save/Save As, and restore them on Open for historical inspection; do not treat restored maps as the next run's assignments. New Start Learning still resamples, new fallback rounds still draw anew, legal coincident draws remain allowed, and no repeated draw is forced solely to differ. Do not add automatic disk writes, duplicate raw source data, manual-seed UI, same-round resampling to improve Loss or claims of cross-hardware bitwise model reproducibility. |

| REQ-128 | Apply the established Learning Settings lifecycle to N and Include Training MSE. Capture both once at Start Learning and lock editing throughout coarse/fine search, Validation admission, Qualification, all fallback rounds and failure/cancellation cleanup; unlock only after the run and cleanup end. Persist configured next-run values through explicit Save/Save As and restore on Open, separately retaining each existing result's captured N, metric mode and associated fold provenance. When only these settings change while idle, apply them to the next Learning run and preserve otherwise-current qualified models and Replay/Export eligibility under their original settings; do not relabel old results, rerun checks implicitly, promote failed/diagnostic results, revive evidence-stale models or bypass active-run/new-run replacement guards. Missing/invalid serialized values remain separately governed. |

| REQ-129 | For otherwise supported project files, restore missing or invalid next-run N to 3 and Include Training MSE to enabled, and visibly report each restored/corrected field. Preserve valid nondefaults. N must be an integer in 2 through 10; an invalid N is reset to 3 rather than clamped. Training inclusion must be a valid boolean. Apply this only to next-run Learning Settings, never rewrite or invent historical kernels' original N, mode, Loss, fold assignments or Validation evidence; absent historical provenance remains unavailable. Do not automatically overwrite the project file, and persist corrections only through explicit Save/Save As. Unsupported schemas and unrelated corrupt source/model data retain their existing handling. |

| REQ-130 | Before evaluating each joint fine-search neighbor, project every out-of-domain coordinate to its nearest allowed endpoint: alpha [1e-6,100], enabled integer Robust K [2,32], and History represented by integer taps from 1 to floor(0.128*sample_rate_hz)+1. History zero means the current-sample tap; do not round the upper History endpoint to an unrealizable duration or expand the 128 ms cap. Apply the same rule at every refinement step, then deduplicate by effective parameters and complete same-round evaluation context. A projected candidate equal to the center or a cached/in-flight candidate does not trigger a duplicate fit; preserve valid changes in other joint coordinates. Boundary arrival alone is not convergence: retain the accepted complete-neighborhood, step-reduction and convergence rules. This concerns generated search candidates, not invalid saved Learning Settings, which retain their default-and-notify policy. |

## Decisions

Chronological decision history. Later explicit supersessions/refinements and the Current resolution index govern; earlier alternatives are not parallel implementation requirements.

| ID | Decision | Rationale |
| --- | --- | --- |
| DEC-001 | Current discussion focuses on reducing late-Cycle prediction MAE/MSE, rather than approving a table-only change. | User supplied four Replay CSV files and explicitly asked how to reduce late-phase loss. No method or implementation is approved. |
| DEC-002 | Adopt phase-aware evaluation and model selection as the improvement method; retain the existing fitting approach and one fixed FIR at deployment. | User explicitly selected 分段評估、選模型 after clarification of its unchanged-fitting and single-model meaning; no numerical target or ranking formula was supplied. |
| DEC-003 | Select the smallest worst-phase MSE first; use overall MSE as the next ranking key when the worst-phase MSE is equal. | User answered 接受 to this exact ordering. The stated tradeoff was that overall average error need not be minimal. |
| DEC-004 | Make the evaluation segment count user-configurable in Learning Settings instead of fixing it to three. Preserve the accepted worst-segment MSE then overall MSE ranking and one fixed deployed FIR. | User explicitly requested choosing the number of segments in Learning Settings; no count range or default was stated. |
| DEC-005 | Maximum configurable evaluation segment count is 10. | User answered 10段 to the maximum-count question. No minimum or default was specified. |
| DEC-006 | Accept original-Cycle equal-duration partition before excluding Training-ineligible data, with fixed boundaries and no time compression, generalized to the configurable count. | User attached 接受 to the prior partition-method question while specifying a maximum of 10; this accepts the partition method, not reinstating a fixed count of three. |
| DEC-007 | Accept segment count range 1-10 with default 3; 1 means whole-Cycle evaluation. | User answered 可以 to the complete range/default proposal, without additional rationale. |
| DEC-008 | Supersede DEC-006: Cycle data must have Ignore removed before phase partition. Configurable count and accepted ranking remain unchanged. | User explicitly corrected the intended order: 我想的是cycle扣除ignore後，再進行分段動作. This is a method correction, not acceptance of the earlier empty-phase proposal. |
| DEC-009 | Remove Collision together with Ignore before constructing evaluation segments. | User answered 對 to removing Collision before segmentation; this refines DEC-008 without changing ranking, range/default, or deployment model. |
| DEC-010 | Accept near-equal retained-sample groups, sizes differing by at most one, with remainder assigned to earlier groups. | User answered 接受 after the example 1000 samples divided into 334,333,333. |
| DEC-011 | Select maximum across Speed x Offset condition worst-phase MSEs, rather than mean, tail mean or a weighted maximum/mean combination. | User explicitly selected 取最大值 after the cross-condition alternatives comparison. No new fitting method or numerical acceptance threshold was selected. |
| DEC-012 | Accept corresponding-phase MSE pooling across Cycles of the same Speed x Offset, weighted by valid sample count with equal XYZ weights. | User answered 接受 to the proposal and equal-size example (0.001,0.002,0.006) giving 0.003. |
| DEC-013 | Accept persisted, run-locked segment settings with stale-result invalidation and explicit retraining after a change. | User answered 接受 to the proposed lifecycle and its setting/result consistency rationale. |
| DEC-014 | Include a segmentation-feasibility message rather than relying only on a later unexplained training failure. | User asked whether the UI can report an unsupported division at segmentation time and whether that calculation is expensive. Timing and enforcement were not yet approved. |
| DEC-015 | Adopt immediate feedback on count edits and a fresh pre-start check before Learning. | User answered 採用 to these two check points; enforcement for partially unsupported histories was not selected. |
| DEC-016 | Supersede DEC-015 timing: check only on Start Learning, present a prompt, and let the user confirm whether to begin Learning. | User annotation explicitly replaces edit-time feedback with a pre-Learning prompt and confirmation. |
| DEC-017 | Unsupported segmentation does not by itself prevent training; only the unavailable region's phase Loss must not be used in evaluation. | User explicitly rejected skipping training on an unsupported partition and separated trainability from loss-evaluation availability. Earlier skip-history and global-block suggestions were never approved. |
| DEC-018 | Extend the requested phase-based selection workflow to automatic retraining when Loss is poor; specific retry settings and policies are not yet selected. | User explicitly said 希望 Loss 不佳時自動再訓練 and asked which parameters can change. This resolves the intent question, not the numerical criterion or retry algorithm. |
| DEC-019 | Select Ridge alpha plus Robust K as the parameters for additional training; investigate the requested gradient approach before choosing the search method. | User explicitly selected Ridge alpha + Robust K and asked whether gradient can find the best values. Alpha-only refinement was a proposal and was not accepted. |
| DEC-020 | Add History to the joint optimization scope and request Loss optimization before detector qualification in each existing fallback round. | User explicitly requested first finding the best alpha, History and K through Loss gradient, then checking Score errors, removing speed/offset as before on failure, and optimizing again. This does not approve a replacement fitting method or relaxed qualification rules. |
| DEC-021 | Accept automatic joint alpha/History/K optimization without mandating gradients for all parameters. | User answered 可以接受 and stated the purpose is automatic discovery of these three parameters; no specific optimizer was selected. |
| DEC-022 | Remove phase-based Loss and its associated proposed segmentation settings/checks; retain maximum across Speed x Offset whole-condition MSE and overall-MSE tie-breaker. | User explicitly annotated loss不用沿用分段. This removes phase subdivision, not the earlier accepted separate-condition maximum, held-out evaluation or fixed FIR. |
| DEC-023 | Select coarse-to-fine derivative-free search as the automatic optimization method. | User answered 可以 to comparing broad parameter combinations then adding candidates around better results. The stated limitation was no guaranteed global optimum and unresolved bounds/stopping conditions. |
| DEC-024 | Require an opportunity to find better History models outside 4-32 ms; the previously proposed fixed envelope was not accepted. | User said 我也想到找到範圍外可能更好的模型 in response to the stated limitation of the proposed History bounds. No particular expanded bound was specified. |
| DEC-025 | Set the maximum History search limit to 128 ms. | User answered 128ms to the maximum permitted History question. This approves the upper bound only, not the lower bound, optimizer budget or a particular optimal History. |
| DEC-026 | Include 0 ms as the History lower bound, completing the accepted 0-128 ms interval. | User answered 可以 to the explicit one-tap/current-sample interpretation and 0-128 ms range. No alpha or K domain was approved. |
| DEC-027 | Accept Ridge alpha bounds 0.000001 through 100 and logarithmic search spacing. | User answered 可以 to this explicit domain/coordinate proposal and its stated outside-domain limitation. No K domain or search budget was approved. |
| DEC-028 | Require exploration of K outside 4-8; the proposed bounded integer 4-8 domain was not accepted. | User answered 想找到範圍外的K. This approves wider exploration only, not numerical bounds, fractional values or an unlimited search. |
| DEC-029 | Record the user-proposed K search bounds as 2-32, subject to the remaining representation and search-policy decisions. | User replied 2~32? to the lower/upper bound question. The feasibility response accepts this as a finite candidate domain, not a claim of measured optimality or approval of fractional K. |
| DEC-030 | Accept integer Robust K within the proposed 2-32 interval. | User said 可以使用整數 and then asked why a range is needed instead of integer gradient search. This confirms representation, not a switch from the accepted coarse-to-fine method to pure local descent. |
| DEC-031 | Accept the combined minimum-step and complete-refinement Loss-improvement convergence policy. | User attached 可以 to 最小步長＋完整細搜後的 Loss 改善幅度 after the explanatory examples. No numerical alpha step or Loss threshold was selected. |
| DEC-032 | Select multiplication/division by 1.05 as the finest alpha step. | User explicitly answered 乘除1.05 instead of accepting the proposed 1.1 example. This selects alpha resolution only, not the Loss-improvement tolerance. |
| DEC-033 | Accept 0.1percent relative best-Loss improvement as the initial material-improvement tolerance. | User answered 可以 to the concrete threshold and example. This only affects optimizer convergence, not detector qualification. |
| DEC-034 | Make the Loss-improvement tolerance configurable instead of hard-coded. | During this turn the user steered with 我認為門檻可以當作參數, referring to the just-accepted Loss-improvement threshold. No change to detector threshold or the combined convergence policy is requested. |
| DEC-035 | Accept common held-out validation sample points across History candidates. | User annotated 不同 History 採用共同驗證資料點比較 Loss，可以嗎 with 可以, after examples distinguishing a common Cycle from common timestamps. The accompanying why-History question asks for explanation, not cancellation of this acceptance. |
| DEC-036 | Accept pre-start tolerance editing and whole-run capture/locking. | User answered 可以. Prior response rendering corruption does not change the already approved default of 0.1 percent. |
| DEC-037 | Accept project save/restore of the configurable Loss-improvement tolerance. | User answered 可以 to retaining a saved 0.05 percent value on reopen instead of resetting to 0.1 percent. This does not approve a missing-field migration or historical-result policy. |
| DEC-038 | Accept defaulting an absent new tolerance field to 0.1 percent in otherwise supported projects. | User answered 可以 to this narrow compatibility behavior and the distinction from historical model provenance. |
| DEC-039 | Accept preservation of qualified results after tolerance-only edits, with original run provenance and next-run application of the new setting. | User answered acceptance and separately reported missing choice controls; the latter is a discussion UI concern, not a model-policy change. |
| DEC-040 | Accept single Loss-winner admission before Qualification and existing fallback; no runner-up shortlist. | User answered 不需要 when asked whether other Loss-ranked candidates should be tested before removing Speed/Offset. |
| DEC-041 | Accept retaining the 128 ms ceiling and skipping an evidence-deficient round with an explanatory message and existing fallback. | User explicitly selected this policy rather than automatic History reduction. |
| DEC-042 | Accept the top three distinct coarse parameter combinations as separate fine-search starts. | User selected 保留粗搜排名前 3 組，分別細搜. |
| DEC-043 | Accept the proposed 210-triple coarse grid. | User answered 可以, then asked how workers should be arranged. This accepts the grid, not an execution policy. |
| DEC-044 | Select coarse maximum eight workers and fine four worker slots instead of the proposed four/three arrangement. | User explicitly requested 粗搜最多 8 個、細搜 4 個 and asked whether one qualified model meant one worker; no increase in fine-search starts is requested. |
| DEC-045 | Accept a shared candidate work queue for four fine-search workers serving three starts, with result barriers before search updates. | User answered 可以 to this explicit scheduling proposal. |
| DEC-046 | Accept maximum-four Recording-level Qualification workers with central bounds/candidate and metric aggregation for one final model. | User answered 可以 to the explicit proposed arrangement. |
| DEC-047 | Reject adaptive memory-driven concurrency reduction; retain hardware-insufficiency reporting and whole-run termination. | User explicitly said 記憶體不足，維持之前說硬體不足，取消工作. This rejects the pending adaptive admission proposal in DISC-046. |
| DEC-048 | Accept the full local neighborhood of at most 26 candidate triples per fine-search start at fixed steps. | User answered 採用 to the proposed joint-neighbor comparison. |
| DEC-049 | Accept the proposed initial fine-search steps and reduction schedule. | User answered 採用 to the History approximately 32 ms, K step 4, alpha ratio 10 proposal and its improvement/no-improvement update rules. |
| DEC-050 | Replace three fine-search starts with four and accept independent convergence of all four before final selection. | User explicitly selected 採用4路各自收斂，四路全部完成才進入最終選模. This changes start count, not the four-worker capacity or one-model Qualification policy. |
| DEC-051 | Select only STD and L2_NORM, rejecting the proposed four-variant comparison. | User selected 標準差 and X、Y、Z合成大小 through annotations and explicitly requested this combination only. |
| DEC-052 | Replace single-winner Qualification admission with retention and Qualification of all best-Loss-tied kernels; reject parameter-based tie elimination. | User said 我的想法是相同loss的kernel都保留，都去做Qualification. This is a change to the earlier single-model policy, not acceptance of DISC-051 ordering. |
| DEC-053 | Use both Loss metrics in their established order and retain all ties on both, rather than primary-only ties. | User clarified 我想要保留最大條件 MSE 跟整體 pooled MSE 最小的。相同都保留 in response to whether different pooled MSE values should both survive; preserve the previously accepted primary-then-pooled ordering. |
| DEC-054 | Replace two-metric tied admission with primary maximum-condition MSE only; retain all kernels at the minimum primary Loss. | User explicitly corrected the policy: 改成最大條件 MSE 的最低loss都保留. |
| DEC-055 | Select four decimal places for rounded best-Loss tie comparison. | User annotated the decimal-precision question with 小數後4位. |
| DEC-056 | Record the requested change to Training-Loss-based model selection with a reserved overfitting check. | User says that after Training, Loss should be computed on all Training data to find the best kernel, and approximately one third to one quarter may be reserved to assess overfitting. This selects a new design direction, not an exact data partition or overfit gate. |
| DEC-057 | Confirm that all Training data means the eligible remainder after setting aside reserved Validation. | User answered 對 to the explicit separation question. This changes the selection objective from held-out CV Loss to post-fit Training Loss without choosing a split ratio or overfit gate. |
| DEC-058 | Exclude every kernel failing reserved Validation from Qualification. | User explicitly confirms: 對，未通過 Validation 檢查的 kernel，不進入 Qualification. Diagnostic-only checking is rejected; no particular failure threshold or all-rejected response is selected. |
| DEC-059 | Require every active condition to pass condition-matched Training versus Validation MSE checks. | User answered 可以 to the explicit proposal 逐條件比較，全部通過才進入 Qualification. Numerical tolerance, relative/absolute formula and zero handling were expressly deferred. |
| DEC-060 | Adopt a configurable percentage allowance for condition-matched Validation deterioration. | User answered 可以 to the explicit percentage-rule and separate Learning Settings proposal. The preceding 20 percent example was expressly not a chosen default; no zero handling or setting lifecycle is inferred. |
| DEC-061 | Choose 20 percent as the default Validation MSE allowed increase. | User explicitly states 預設20%. This is now an accepted default rather than the previously illustrative value, without claiming empirical calibration or overfit absence. |
| DEC-062 | Retain the multiplicative Validation criterion at zero and near-zero Training MSE, with no absolute allowance. | User answered 可以 to the explicit proposal. This includes unrounded gate inputs and does not authorize converting numerical errors to ordinary Validation rejection. |
| DEC-063 | Adopt existing scope fallback after all retained best-Training-Loss kernels fail Validation. | After asking what fallback meant and receiving the verified speed/offset example, user explicitly answered 採用. The acceptance adds this gate-failure trigger without changing the existing removal order or prior-qualified retention. |
| DEC-064 | Select complete-Cycle partitioning for all conditions, including conditions with multiple recordings. | User explicitly states 都用完整的cycle分配, rejecting the proposed whole-recording priority. This changes the new split policy without requesting individual-row mixing or restoring held-out CV as the Training-Loss objective. |
| DEC-065 | Accept per-condition Validation Cycle count floor(n/3). | User annotated the one-third downward-rounding proposal with 採用. The explicitly illustrated five-Cycle deviation is part of the accepted count rule. |
| DEC-066 | Require each participating recording to retain at least one Training Cycle. | User explicitly adds 要保證每個recordining 至少要有一個cycle進行training. This constrains selection while retaining the accepted per-condition Validation count. |
| DEC-067 | Adopt fixed-seed constrained random Cycle selection and shared candidate evidence. | After clarification and an acknowledgement-only turn, user explicitly answered 可以 to the proposal listing the per-condition count, per-recording Training minimum, shared candidate partition and reproducibility for unchanged inputs. |
| DEC-068 | Choose new Cycle sampling for each fallback round instead of whole-run frozen assignments. | User answers 重新抽樣 to the explicit whole-run-freeze proposal. This changes fallback assignment lifecycle, not the already accepted fixed-seed reproducibility, per-condition count, per-recording Training minimum or common evidence within a round. |
| DEC-069 | Allow coincidentally repeated Cycle partitions after fallback resampling. | User answered 可以 to the explicit repeated-outcome question. Resampling remains required each round, but its result need not differ from previous rounds. |
| DEC-070 | Adopt the same validated fitted kernel directly into Qualification, without an intervening combined-data refit. | After the three-Cycle clarification, user explicitly answers 了解，採用. The acceptance does not remove Training-internal robust refitting or later fallback optimization. |
| DEC-071 | Retain robust-rejected eligible points in Training Loss evaluation. | User says 採用, then separately questions whether fitting rejection should be removed because IMU data are noisy. Only the evaluation policy is accepted. |
| DEC-072 | Remove Robust rejection/refit and its K optimization dimension; use full-eligible-Training Ridge with alpha/History optimization. | User explicitly states 我認為取消Robust refit，因為已經有ignore了。Robust refit 有點像是對資料進行濾波了 in response to the informed removal question. Preserve this rationale while clarifying that current refit selects fitting rows rather than filtering raw waveforms. |
| DEC-073 | Make Robust refit optional in Learning Settings, default off, rather than removing the capability entirely. | User annotates the plain-Ridge proposal with 對。不然在learning settings增加是否使用Robust refit。預設取消. This latest steering retains plain Ridge as default and restores the previously designed robust branch when explicitly enabled. |
| DEC-074 | Lock the Robust refit setting for the entire Learning run. | User answered 可以 to the explicit whole-run lock proposal, with changes reserved for the next Learning run. |
| DEC-075 | Save and restore the Robust refit option with its project. | User answered 可以 to the explicit project-persistence question, including new-project default off and preservation of model-specific original mode. |
| DEC-076 | Extend common History-supported target timestamps to post-fit Training Loss while retaining common reserved-Validation timestamps. | User says 可以，我目前的認知是History是FIR的深度, then asks 是因為延遲所以才要這樣比較嗎? The acceptance is recorded; the factual depth/latency questions do not select a time shift or fixed latency. |
| DEC-077 | Preserve otherwise-current qualified results after an idle Robust refit-only setting edit. | User answered 可以 to preserving prior qualified kernels with their original mode for Replay/Export while the new setting applies only to later Learning. |
| DEC-078 | Lock the Validation MSE allowance for the whole Learning run. | User answered 可以 to the explicit whole-run lock proposal, including all fallback rounds and editing after termination. |

## Acceptance Criteria

Apply each validation method to its surviving requirement under the Current resolution index. For superseded portions, verify the documented replacement and absence of obsolete behavior rather than implementing historical examples. Evidence below is planned, not test results.

| ID | Requirements | Validation Method | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| AC-001 | REQ-001 | Traceability review under the Current resolution index: Historical scope clarification, closed by the confirmed Solution. The user confirms the improvement scope before a final proposal or implementation. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-002 | REQ-002 | Traceability review under the Current resolution index: Historical scope clarification, closed by the confirmed Solution. A future candidate must be compared using explicit phase boundaries and held-out Cycle evidence; numerical targets and allowable regressions require confirmation. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-003 | REQ-003 | Traceability review under the Current resolution index: Superseded phase requirement; REQ-023 removes this feature. Verify candidate fitting remains unchanged and the finally selected kernel is one fixed FIR; phase scores and ranking must be reproducible under the subsequently confirmed rule. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-004 | REQ-004 | Traceability review under the Current resolution index: Superseded phase requirement; REQ-023 removes this feature. In a deterministic example with phase MSEs A=(0.001,0.001,0.006) and B=(0.002,0.002,0.003), B ranks before A; tied worst-phase MSEs are ordered by overall MSE. Final same-key tie handling remains unresolved. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-005 | REQ-005 | Traceability review under the Current resolution index: Superseded phase requirement; REQ-023 removes this feature. For two permitted counts, verify the selected setting reaches held-out evaluation, produces the expected number of segments and controls the worst-segment ranking input; invalid and persisted-value behavior requires the subsequently confirmed setting contract. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-006 | REQ-006 | Traceability review under the Current resolution index: Superseded phase requirement; REQ-023 removes this feature. Verify 10 is accepted and values above 10 are rejected by the eventual UI/request/persistence contract; verify default and lower-bound cases once decided. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-007 | REQ-007 | Traceability review under the Current resolution index: Superseded phase requirement; REQ-023 removes this feature. For a 9-second Cycle and count 3, phase boundaries remain at relative times 0,3,6,9 seconds even when an event exclusion removes middle-phase samples; for count 10 verify equal fractions of the same original duration. Do not restart or partition FIR fitting at evaluation boundaries. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-008 | REQ-008 | Traceability review under the Current resolution index: Superseded phase requirement; REQ-023 removes this feature. Verify a new setting defaults to 3, integers 1 and 10 are accepted, 0/11/fractional values are rejected, and count 1 produces a whole-Cycle phase while preserving eligibility and fixed FIR behavior. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-009 | REQ-009 | Traceability review under the Current resolution index: Superseded phase requirement; REQ-023 removes this feature. At a uniform sample rate, a 9-second Cycle with Ignore covering relative times [3,6) leaves 6 seconds for a 3-way partition: each phase covers 2 seconds of retained data, potentially discontiguous in original time. Verify source indices/timestamps and FIR support remain unchanged; the final prepartition Collision policy and remainder handling must be specified before implementing the general fixture. Supersede AC-007 fixed-original-time boundaries. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-010 | REQ-010 | Traceability review under the Current resolution index: Superseded phase requirement; REQ-023 removes this feature. Verify both annotated Ignore and Collision samples are absent from the partition pool, their original indices remain traceable, and samples outside those annotations are not removed merely because they lie within the collision response window. Preserve the original detector timeline. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-011 | REQ-011 | Traceability review under the Current resolution index: Superseded phase requirement; REQ-023 removes this feature. For 1000 retained samples and P=3 verify lengths 334,333,333; for 1001 verify 334,334,333; for 1000 and P=10 verify ten groups of 100. Assert full one-to-one ordered sample coverage, no ignored/collision samples, and no FIR-history splicing. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-012 | REQ-012 | Traceability review under the Current resolution index: Superseded phase requirement; REQ-023 removes this feature. With condition worst-phase values A=(0.004,0.008) and B=(0.005,0.006), rank B before A using 0.006 versus 0.008; tied maxima defer to overall MSE. Verify excluded-round conditions cannot affect this selection. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-013 | REQ-013 | Traceability review under the Current resolution index: Superseded phase requirement; REQ-023 removes this feature. Equal-count Cycle phases with MSEs 0.001,0.002,0.006 yield pooled 0.003; 100 and 200 samples with MSEs 0.001 and 0.004 yield 0.003 rather than 0.0025. Verify each held-out XYZ residual contributes once to its condition/phase and cross-condition maximum remains unchanged. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-014 | REQ-014 | Traceability review under the Current resolution index: Superseded phase requirement; REQ-023 removes this feature. Save/reopen restores the selected count; attempts to change it during an active run leave the captured request unchanged. A changed value after completion marks results stale and prevents their export as current until a new run; setting the same value does not invalidate results or trigger training. Verify no automatic Learning starts. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-015 | REQ-015 | Traceability review under the Current resolution index: Superseded phase requirement; REQ-023 removes this feature. Verify a synthetic unsupported count produces clear Recording/Cycle/history/count provenance before expensive work under the subsequently accepted preflight timing. Count-only checks must agree with existing FIR eligibility and must not fit models or evaluate loss merely to count rows. Full UI latency is to be measured after integration. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-016 | REQ-016 | Traceability review under the Current resolution index: Superseded phase requirement; REQ-023 removes this feature. Changing count updates feedback without running model fitting. Changing annotations/data invalidates stale feasibility results; Start Learning checks current inputs before expensive work. Verify displayed provenance and actual eligibility agree, and measure full UI responsiveness after integration rather than relying on the arithmetic prototype. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-017 | REQ-017 | Traceability review under the Current resolution index: Superseded phase requirement; REQ-023 removes this feature. Changing count does not run segmentation preflight. Clicking Start Learning computes and displays current partition availability before a worker starts; cancel leaves Learning unstarted, confirm permits normal training eligibility checks and launch. Confirmations must refer to the displayed current settings/data. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-018 | REQ-018 | Traceability review under the Current resolution index: Superseded phase requirement; REQ-023 removes this feature. A fixture with otherwise valid training evidence but insufficient phase coverage still reaches fitting after confirmation. Unavailable phase metrics are explicitly missing, not numeric zero; available phase contributions retain their accepted pooling. Verify no recording/history is dropped solely because of segmentation. Preserve existing genuine training-invalid input failures. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-019 | REQ-019 | Traceability review under the Current resolution index: Historical optimizer direction, refined by REQ-024 and the Current resolution index. After policy confirmation, verify the accepted poor-Loss condition triggers only permitted new training attempts, obeys stop/resource limits and handles unavailable phase metrics explicitly. Compare candidates under the accepted fixed evaluation contract and preserve qualification gates; no improvement is presumed without held-out evidence. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-020 | REQ-020 | Traceability review under the Current resolution index: Historical optimizer direction, refined by REQ-024 and the Current resolution index. Once the search method is confirmed, verify both parameters can vary within the approved domains, candidate comparisons use identical held-out evidence and phase definitions, and chosen values minimize the accepted ranking among evaluated valid candidates. Do not claim a global optimum or qualify a model merely because it wins the search. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-021 | REQ-021 | Traceability review under the Current resolution index: Historical optimizer direction, refined by REQ-024 and the Current resolution index. After algorithm and candidate-admission decisions are confirmed, verify each changed Speed/Offset scope causes fresh optimization under the accepted held-out phase-Loss objective before detector qualification. Verify all three parameter choices are traceable, qualification is not used as the differentiable Loss objective, and fallback does not qualify a model merely because its Loss is lowest. Preserve existing 150 ms, Ignore and C<N behavior rules and retained qualified results. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-022 | REQ-022 | Traceability review under the Current resolution index: Historical optimizer direction, refined by REQ-024 and the Current resolution index. Once the optimizer is selected, verify it considers all three approved parameter domains, records evaluated values and objective outcomes, and preserves optimize-then-qualify ordering after each fallback. No requirement assumes a gradient exists for integer tap count or hard rejection. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-023 | REQ-023 | Traceability review under the Current resolution index: Active removal of segmentation; later CV objective, optional Robust and multi-kernel rules supersede older restatements in this row. Verify whole-condition held-out MSE equals pooled SSE divided by XYZ observation count, the largest active-condition MSE is primary and overall MSE breaks ties. Verify no evaluation partition count or partition failure influences fitting or ranking, no phase UI/checks are added, and existing annotation eligibility, grouped folds, history boundaries and detector gates remain unchanged. This replaces the earlier phase-specific acceptance criteria. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-024 | REQ-024 | After detailed policy confirmation, verify broad initial evaluation precedes local refinement, new candidates use the same accepted held-out objective, duplicate effective parameter combinations are not treated as new evidence, and approved limits stop additional work. Verify no gradient through discrete taps or hard rejection is required. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-025 | REQ-025 | After expanded-domain policy confirmation, verify candidates outside the old envelope can be evaluated within the approved hard limits and compared under the same Loss contract. Use a synthetic objective with a better outer region to verify that policy does not falsely claim the original envelope covers the optimum; resource and stopping limits remain enforced. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-026 | REQ-026 | Verify the candidate-domain logic permits the 128 ms boundary when realizable, excludes effective histories exceeding it, and maps 128 ms at 1000 Hz to 129 taps. At other sample rates verify the subsequently confirmed integer-tap policy respects the cap and deduplicates equivalent histories. This arithmetic does not establish runtime feasibility or model quality. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-027 | REQ-027 | Verify 0 ms maps to one FIR tap and still reaches normal fitting/evaluation; at 1000 Hz the permitted effective History domain spans 0-128 ms with tap counts 1-129. Reject candidates outside the approved range, deduplicate equal tap counts and preserve eligibility and qualification checks. Domain membership does not imply every tap count is exhaustively evaluated. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-028 | REQ-028 | Verify candidate alphas are positive and within the inclusive bounds, intermediate values are permitted, and refinement uses the approved logarithmic coordinate. Each candidate is assessed with the accepted complete fit/refit and held-out Loss contract. | Pending implementation and verification. |

| AC-048 | REQ-048 | Inject an insufficient-memory admission result, an explicit worker MemoryError and a runtime aggregate-memory-limit breach. | AC-050 | REQ-050 | At 1000 Hz verify initial tap step 32 and no-improvement reductions 32,16,8,4,2,1; K steps 4,2,1; and alpha repeated square roots clamped to 1.05. | AC-052 | REQ-052 | Exercise four starts with differing improvement histories and completion order. | AC-054 | REQ-054 | Once the tie definition is confirmed, supply multiple distinct best-Loss-tied kernels and a worse-Loss kernel. Verify global worker limits, hardware-failure cancellation and current per-model threshold/evidence guards remain enforced. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-055 | REQ-055 | For illustrative metric pairs A=(0.010,0.006), B=(0.010,0.006), C=(0.010,0.008), D=(0.012,0.005), verify A and B are retained together, C is excluded by secondary MSE and D by primary MSE despite its smaller pooled MSE. Different History/alpha/K values or completion order must not discard A or B. Test under the separately confirmed numerical-equality policy. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-056 | REQ-056 | For illustrative metric pairs A=(0.010,0.006), B=(0.010,0.006), C=(0.010,0.008), D=(0.012,0.005), retain A/B/C and send each through the existing Qualification pipeline; exclude D despite its smaller pooled MSE. Verify no tied kernel is eliminated by pooled MSE, History, alpha, K or completion order, while existing validity/bounds and global worker limits remain enforced. | AC-060 | REQ-060 | With the subsequently approved Validation criterion, verify that failing members of a best-Training-Loss tied set never enter collision Qualification, while passing members remain eligible subject to existing bounds and qualification guards. Record each validation result and reason. Do not silently qualify rejected kernels or admit worse-Training-Loss runners-up when all fail. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-061 | REQ-061 | After the deterioration criterion is approved, verify that any failing active Speed x Offset prevents kernel admission even when independently aggregated maximum MSEs satisfy the same nominal limit. Verify all required conditions pass before admission and retain per-condition provenance. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-062 | REQ-062 | With an explicitly supplied test setting p=20 and positive Training MSE T=0.010, verify the proposed inclusive bound is 0.012, so V=0.011 passes and V=0.013 fails; test the exact boundary under the subsequently approved numeric comparison. Twenty percent is test input, not a default. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-063 | REQ-063 | Verify a new settings instance initializes the Validation increase allowance to 20 percent and displays that value independently of other thresholds. Verify user-selected valid values replace the default rather than hard-coding 20 in evaluation. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-064 | REQ-064 | Verify T=0,V=0 passes, T=0 with finite positive V fails, and tiny positive baselines use the same relative upper bound without clipping or an absolute floor. Verify four-decimal selection rounding cannot change gate inputs and no division-by-zero occurs in the gate; invalid/nonfinite evidence must not be treated as an ordinary finite comparison. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-065 | REQ-065 | Test an all-Validation-rejected tied set produces a Validation-failed reason and no collision Qualification calls, then advances from speeds 500/1000 with offsets 4/5/7/8 to speed 1000 with the same offsets; a subsequent rejection advances to all speeds with offsets 5/7/8. Verify renewed optimization, original recordings retained, earlier qualified results preserved, configured allowance unchanged and no higher-Loss candidate admission. Verify eventual scope exhaustion follows existing results behavior and memory/numerical failures retain their separate handling. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-066 | REQ-066 | Verify Cycle assignment is disjoint using recording_id plus cycle_index for single- and multi-recording conditions, including repeated local cycle indexes across recordings. Every eligible row of a Cycle follows its assigned role; Collision/Ignore remain excluded and no cross-Cycle FIR history is fabricated. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-067 | REQ-067 | For per-condition totals n=3,4,5,6,8 verify Validation counts 1,1,1,2,2 and Training counts 2,3,4,4,6, subject to other evidence requirements. | AC-068 | REQ-068 | Verify every recording has at least one valid Training Cycle after partition and per-condition Validation counts remain floor(n/3). Add infeasible-outcome tests once that policy is settled. | Pending implementation and verification. |

Verify all coarse/fine candidate workers consume the same assignments without independent random draws or completion-order effects. Do not require every seed change to alter the assignment, a per-recording Validation quota or uniform selection probabilities. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-070 | REQ-070 | Verify every newly entered fallback round generates and records its own constrained assignment before candidate evaluation, including rounds restoring a speed. | AC-071 | REQ-071 | Use a controlled selector returning a previously observed legal partition for a later round and verify the result is accepted without uniqueness retries, no constraint is relaxed and each round retains separate assignment/model provenance. | AC-073 | REQ-073 | For 1000 otherwise eligible Training evaluation points and 50 points excluded only from robust refitting, verify all 1000 contribute to post-fit Training Loss while only 950 contribute to that robust refit. | AC-074 | REQ-074 | Verify candidate fitting uses all its otherwise eligible Training rows without a residual-rejection pass or second robust fit; large finite residuals alone must not remove a fitting row. Verify Collision/Ignore and reserved Validation exclusions, existing FIR support and finite checks remain. Score normalization must use Training evidence and preserve STD/L2. Ensure no active K changes candidate predictions or search, and historical evidence is not relabeled. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-075 | REQ-075 | Verify the 35 requested coarse pairs before sample-realization deduplication, absence of a K loop, and at most eight unique two-coordinate neighbors per fine start. Do not assert measured runtime improvement from candidate counts alone. | Pending implementation and verification. |

Add capture/persistence/stale-result tests after those policies are approved; do not relabel historical models. | Pending implementation and verification. |
| AC-077 | REQ-077 | Verify mode-dependent coarse counts of 35 requested pairs versus 210 requested triples before alias deduplication and neighbor maxima of eight versus 26. | AC-078 | REQ-078 | Verify both on/off modes are captured at Start Learning, editing is prevented throughout all active stages and fallback, and all workers receive the captured mode. | AC-079 | REQ-079 | Verify round-trip Save/Open and Save As/Open restore both enabled and disabled values, and a new project defaults disabled. Verify no nested per-candidate/fold worker pools, and preserve terminal hardware/worker errors and the four-recording-worker Qualification ceiling. Use controlled fixtures; no unmeasured speedup guarantee. | Pending implementation and verification. |

| AC-120 | REQ-120 | Once the remaining comparator/pool/gate policies are confirmed, verify nominal selection of the two lowest eligible fold-kernel Loss entries, without an additional combined-data model fit. Verify selected kernels retain exact fitted model/calibration and their own fold evidence, while parameter-level CV ranking still uses all required folds. Cover cutoff ties, multiple tied parameter configurations, insufficient survivors and Validation rejection under the subsequently confirmed policies; do not imply those outcomes are settled by this criterion. | Pending implementation and verification. |

| AC-121 | REQ-121 | Verify same-condition T=0.10 permits V=0.12 at p=20 and rejects V>0.12; verify T=0 only permits V=0 and a failing required condition rejects that fold kernel regardless of other conditions. Verify valid finite rejected folds remain in the N-fold parameter Loss, while downstream selection excludes them before taking two eligible fold kernels. Use fixed distinct fold-kernel Loss inputs to exercise admission order without assuming the unresolved comparator/pool rules. Preserve no final refit, no automatic threshold relaxation and no higher-loss parameter promotion. | Pending implementation and verification. |

| AC-122 | REQ-122 | With two parameter configurations tied at the minimum rounded CV Loss and each providing at least two passing distinct-score fold kernels, verify selection contains two from each, even if both of the globally smallest fold-kernel scores belong to one configuration. Verify Validation failures are filtered within each configuration and no additional combined-data refit or increased global worker concurrency occurs. Cutoff ties and fewer-than-two survivors are tested only after their separate policies are adopted. | Pending implementation and verification. |

| AC-123 | REQ-123 | For three passing fold kernels in one retained configuration with condition Losses A=(0.10,0.70), B=(0.45,0.45), C=(0.38,0.48), verify worst-condition values 0.70,0.45,0.48 and selection B,C. Verify both Training-inclusion modes feed their declared L_fc values, parameter selection still uses max of condition fold means, and no cross-condition mean/sample pooling or extra fit is introduced. Explanations must not imply that the selected kernels have the largest ranking values. Precision/cutoff ties remain separately governed. | Pending implementation and verification. |

| AC-124 | REQ-124 | Verify passing kernels with worst-condition values 0.4000,0.4500,0.4500 all survive and that 0.4501 does not share the second-place tie. Verify decimal half-up boundaries (0.12345 -> 0.1235), retained original raw values, and selection of all passing kernels tied with the rounded second cutoff even when their raw values differ. Verify filtering precedes this selection and that neither gate inputs nor coarse/fine search comparisons are rounded. Apply checks independently per retained parameter configuration; no extra worker concurrency. | Pending implementation and verification. |

| AC-125 | REQ-125 | With tied best parameter groups A,B, verify A has one passing kernel and B none -> only A's unchanged kernel proceeds, without fallback for the zero-survivor B alone. Verify A and B both have zero passing kernels -> Validation-failed round and existing fallback, with no Qualification jobs for them. Verify >=2 survivors use the adopted rounded-second-place tie rule, and no higher-Loss parameter group, rejected kernel, resampling, new fit or relaxed gate fills shortages. Preserve distinct terminal Cycle/allocation and infrastructure failures. | Pending implementation and verification. |

| AC-126 | REQ-126 | Verify per-condition n=7,N=3 gives a permutation of 3,2,2 held-out Cycle counts, n=4,N=3 gives 2,1,1, and divisible counts are equal. Validate exact-once held-out membership, no Cycle splitting, required condition Validation coverage and recording Training retention. For two recordings with two Cycles each at N=3, verify a valid 2/1/1 assignment such as (A1,B1)/(A2)/(B2). Ensure unequal Cycle lengths do not silently substitute sample-count balancing, and all same-round candidates share the accepted assignment. | Pending implementation and verification. |

| AC-127 | REQ-127 | Verify a new Start Learning requests a fresh sampling seed rather than reusing restored result state, same-round candidates share one recorded map, and new fallback rounds use their declared round-specific draw. With fixed source identities, sampler version and seed inputs, verify fold membership reconstruction matches the captured actual map. Verify Save/Save As/Open round-trip seed, scopes, N and actual recording/Cycle/fold assignments without duplicate raw data or automatic disk writes; Open alone must not seed the next active run from historical assignments. Do not assert that randomly generated legal partitions must differ or that model floating-point outputs are bitwise reproducible across hardware. | Pending implementation and verification. |

| AC-128 | REQ-128 | Verify both controls cannot change active-run values during coarse/fine, admission, Qualification, fallback or cleanup and unlock afterward. Verify Save/Open preserves configured next-run values while a previously qualified result retains its original different N/mode and actual fold metadata. Verify idle setting-only edits preserve otherwise-current result eligibility without relabeling metrics, reviving stale/failed results or changing export/new-run guards. Missing/invalid serialization cases await their separate policy. | Pending implementation and verification. |

| AC-129 | REQ-129 | Verify supported projects with missing/invalid next-run N or Training-inclusion fields open with N=3/on respectively and report corrected fields; preserve valid N=5/off. Test out-of-range and fractional N, invalid boolean representations and absent fields. Verify corrections neither modify historical result settings/fold maps/Loss nor fabricate missing evidence, and do not write to disk until Save. Verify this narrow policy does not suppress unrelated unsupported-schema or corrupt-model errors. | Pending implementation and verification. |

| AC-130 | REQ-130 | Test alpha 99*1.05 -> 100 and a below-minimum proposal -> 1e-6; enabled K=31+4 -> 32 and below 2 -> 2. At 1 kHz test History 120+32 ms -> 129 taps (128 ms), and at a rate with a nonintegral 128 ms sample count verify the maximum realizable tap endpoint never exceeds the duration cap. Check lower History bound is one tap, projection precedes effective deduplication, center/duplicate/in-flight candidates are not refitted, other valid joint-coordinate changes survive, and endpoint arrival does not bypass full-sweep convergence. Disabled Robust mode does not introduce K. Check saved invalid N still resets to 3 rather than using the search projection policy. | Pending implementation and verification. |

## Relationships

The narrative below records chronology. Earlier references to pending choices and to unchanged legacy minimums were subsequently resolved by the Current resolution index; they are not current exceptions or open conflicts.

DEC-001 resolves the discussion focus of REQ-001; REQ-002 refines that focus. DEC-002 refines DEC-001 by selecting the method, and REQ-003 refines REQ-002; those initial refinements did not supersede a decision. DEC-008 subsequently supersedes DEC-006 on partition order. DEC-003 refines DEC-002 by selecting ranking priority; REQ-004 refines REQ-003. DEC-004 refines DEC-002 and REQ-005 refines REQ-003 by adding configurable segment count; it replaces only an unconfirmed fixed-three proposal, not an accepted decision. DEC-005 refines DEC-004 with an upper bound; DEC-006 refines DEC-002 with the accepted time-partition method. REQ-006 refines REQ-005 and REQ-007 refines REQ-003. DEC-007 refines DEC-005 with the lower bound and default; REQ-008 refines REQ-006. Existing SPEC-0001 evidence requirements and SPEC-0009 fixed 150 ms window remain unchanged; this discussion does not supersede them.

DEC-057/REQ-059 supersede only the held-out-CV selection data-source component of DEC-022/REQ-023. Maximum-condition MSE aggregation remains active; common-History support and evidence-shortage rules require explicit adaptation rather than silent removal.
DEC-058/REQ-060 refine DEC-057/REQ-059 and the admission aspect of REQ-056: all minimum rounded Training-Loss ties are retained for Validation, but only passing kernels may proceed to collision Qualification. No change to fitting, objective aggregation or collision qualification criteria is implied.
DEC-059/REQ-061 refine DEC-058/REQ-060 with condition-matched all-conditions validation. They do not supersede maximum-condition Training-Loss selection, rounded tied retention or qualification over all supplied conditions.
DEC-060/REQ-062 refine DEC-059/REQ-061 with the separate configurable percentage criterion for positive non-negligible Training MSE. Default and zero/near-zero behavior remain open, without changing existing collision or convergence thresholds.
DEC-061/REQ-063 refine DEC-060/REQ-062 by selecting the 20 percent default; the setting remains configurable and independent of convergence and collision thresholds.
DEC-062/REQ-064 refine DEC-060/REQ-062 by extending the multiplicative rule to zero and near-zero finite MSE without an absolute floor; they preserve default 20 percent, configurable allowance, and existing numerical-failure policy.
DEC-063/REQ-065 refine DEC-058/REQ-060 and the existing optimization/fallback sequence by adding all-Validation-rejected as an ordinary unsuccessful-scope trigger. They preserve minimum-Training-Loss-only admission, existing scope order and distinct numerical/resource failure behavior.
DEC-064/REQ-066 refine DEC-057/REQ-059 and supersede the inherited whole-recording-priority partition component for this new Training/Validation design only. This is an intended change from current source/manifest behavior, not permission to alter historical project/model evidence or the existing implemented CV workflow before execution authorization.
DEC-065/REQ-067 refine DEC-064/REQ-066 with per-condition integer counts. DEC-066/REQ-068 refine the same partition by imposing at least one Training Cycle per recording. Neither requires Validation evidence from every recording nor changes minimum valid-Cycle input requirements or authorizes an infeasible-partition fallback.
DEC-067/REQ-069 refine DEC-064/REQ-066, DEC-065/REQ-067 and DEC-066/REQ-068 by selecting reproducible constrained Cycle sampling and common candidate partitions. They do not restore CV-based selection or choose a new input-evidence threshold.
DEC-068/REQ-070 refine DEC-067/REQ-069 and REQ-065: shared candidate partitions mean within the same round; every fallback round resamples with reproducible round-specific inputs. They reject only the proposed whole-run freeze, not fixed-seed sampling or disjoint Training/Validation within a round.
DEC-069/REQ-071 refine DEC-068/REQ-070 by allowing repeated legal outcomes, without changing the requirement to draw per round or the accepted reproducibility and Training-retention constraints.
DEC-070/REQ-072 resolve the post-Validation final-refit question under DEC-056/REQ-058 and REQ-059, and refine admission under REQ-060. They supersede any inherited final-fit-on-all-eligible-data wording only insofar as that would merge the reserved Validation subset back into the fitted kernel after its check.
DEC-071/REQ-073 refine DEC-057/REQ-059 by separating robust fitting rejection from Training-Loss eligibility. Removal of robust rejection and K search remains pending DISC-070.
DEC-072/REQ-074 supersede the robust-refit preservation components of REQ-023, REQ-036, REQ-053 and REQ-072, and the K-optimization components of REQ-021/REQ-022/REQ-024. They preserve the other data, objective, normalization and frozen-model safeguards. REQ-073 remains an all-eligible-evaluation invariant; there are no robust-rejected rows under the newly selected fitting method. Historical requirements and model records remain historical.

DEC-074/REQ-078 refine DEC-073/REQ-076 by defining whole-run capture and editing boundaries, without choosing project persistence or result-invalidation rules.
DEC-075/REQ-079 refine DEC-073/REQ-076 with project save/restore while retaining REQ-078 run locking and model-provenance separation.
DEC-076/REQ-080 refine DEC-035/REQ-036 and REQ-059/REQ-073 for the post-fit Training-Loss objective. They supersede only an interpretation of all-Training evaluation that would score different History-specific timestamp sets, while preserving full candidate-specific fitting eligibility and both selectable fitting modes.
DEC-077/REQ-081 refine DEC-073/REQ-076, REQ-078 and REQ-079 with mode-only result preservation. They do not replace existing data-evidence invalidation or automatically qualify models under changed evidence.
DEC-078/REQ-082 refine REQ-062/REQ-063 with Validation allowance capture and editing boundaries. They do not choose persistence or post-run result-eligibility behavior.
DEC-079/REQ-083 refine REQ-062 and REQ-082 with project persistence while preserving REQ-063 default and per-result original threshold provenance.
DEC-080/REQ-084 refine REQ-062/REQ-082/REQ-083 with idle allowance-only result preservation under the original threshold, without substituting new settings for prior qualification evidence.
DEC-081/REQ-085 refine REQ-056/REQ-057 and REQ-052 final-selection population, preserving four fine starts without imposing a four-model final-admission cap.
DEC-082/REQ-086 supersede the exactly-four-start limitation of REQ-051/REQ-052/REQ-075/REQ-077 only when tied coarse candidates expand admission. Preserve their four-worker capacity, independent all-admitted-start convergence and final-selection barrier. The proposed parameter tie-breaker in DISC-079 was rejected, not implemented.

DEC-083/REQ-087 partially supersede DEC-044/REQ-045 and four-fine-worker capacity clauses in REQ-046/REQ-051/REQ-075/REQ-077/REQ-086 and AC-086: fine capacity becomes eight, while the separate Qualification cap remains four. DEC-084/REQ-088 supersede the nominal four-start count in DEC-050/REQ-051 and downstream projections, preserving DEC-082 tie-retention intent subject to DISC-082 boundary clarification. Historical discussion records retain their original proposals.

DEC-085/REQ-089 resolve the pending cutoff semantics in REQ-086/REQ-088 and AC-088, refining DEC-082 tie preservation and DEC-084 top-two admission without changing REQ-087 concurrency or REQ-085 final population.

DEC-086/REQ-090 resolve the halfway convention pending in REQ-057 and DISC-083; they refine REQ-085 final rounded-best retention without changing REQ-089 raw coarse ranking, raw convergence or the Validation gate.

DEC-087/REQ-091 refine REQ-085 candidate identity and REQ-089 fine-start admission by deduplicating identical effective parameters/context, not by eliminating different-parameter Loss ties. They preserve REQ-087 concurrency, independent all-start convergence, fitting-mode semantics and hardware-failure policy.

DEC-088/REQ-092 resolve the exact-zero case pending in REQ-034 while refining REQ-032/REQ-052/REQ-089 convergence. Preserve the strictly-lower move and no-lower-neighbor step reduction in REQ-050, REQ-064 Validation behavior and numerical validity requirements.

DEC-089/REQ-093 resolve post-Qualification tied-kernel retention pending in REQ-054/REQ-085, extending all-ties admission through both gates without a single-representative reduction. They preserve REQ-091 candidate identity and existing fallback, export eligibility and condition-coverage constraints.

DEC-090/REQ-094 adapt REQ-042's missing-common-evidence policy to the post-fit Training and reserved Validation masks of REQ-080, preserving REQ-069/REQ-070 partition lifecycle. They do not alter minimum Cycle counts, candidate fitting eligibility or numerical/resource-failure classification.

DEC-091/REQ-095 replace the unaccepted DISC-088 three-Cycle-per-recording proposal and govern the redesigned input evidence minimum in place of the inspected legacy three-Cycle planning check. REQ-096 resolves count/allocation infeasibility pending in REQ-068/REQ-069 while preserving REQ-067 floor(n/3) and per-recording Training retention. It expressly distinguishes terminal Cycle shortage from REQ-094 otherwise-feasible-split common-evaluation shortage; neither is hardware failure.

DEC-092/REQ-097 refine REQ-096's terminal Cycle-count/allocation policy with Start Learning preflight and consolidated reporting. They preserve REQ-095 minimum evidence and REQ-094 distinct common-target shortage handling, without restoring superseded phase-segmentation UI checks.

DEC-093/REQ-098 complete REQ-035's convergence input domain and refine persistence validation under REQ-038/REQ-039 without changing REQ-034 stopping comparison or REQ-092 exact-zero behavior.

DEC-094/REQ-099 complete the editable Validation allowance domain pending in REQ-062/REQ-083 while preserving REQ-063 default, REQ-064 gate, REQ-082 capture/locking and REQ-084 historical-result eligibility.

DEC-095/REQ-100 complete absent-field defaults under REQ-079/REQ-083 consistently with REQ-039. Preserve REQ-081/REQ-084 original-result provenance and eligibility, and leave present-invalid-field handling to DISC-093.

DEC-096/REQ-101 replace the unaccepted rejection proposal in DISC-093 and supersede any prior prohibition on correcting present invalid new next-run settings under REQ-039/REQ-100. The correction is explicitly user-authorized and reported, not silent; missing fields still follow REQ-100, and historical model/schema validity guards remain unchanged.

DEC-097/REQ-102 refine REQ-050 center moves and REQ-089 all-path completion for tied improving fine neighbors. Preserve REQ-034/REQ-098 material-improvement stopping, REQ-091 shared evaluation, REQ-092 zero-Loss behavior and REQ-085/REQ-093 final tied retention. This is distinct from, and does not expand, the accepted no-improvement plateau step rule.

DEC-098/REQ-103 refine REQ-073/REQ-074/REQ-076 with explicit final-model STD calibration rows, preserving REQ-080 separate common evaluation masks, fixed STD/L2 Score and same-validated-model Qualification. Target numeric evaluation/serialization semantics remain pending DISC-096.

DEC-099/REQ-104 refine REQ-080 comparative evaluation, REQ-091 cache/model identity and REQ-103 calibration with explicit target-numeric semantics; preserve same-validated-model admission, float64 fitting, raw Loss definition and separate Training/Validation roles. Any legacy assumptions that selection may use unconverted float64 predictions are superseded for the new optimizer.

DEC-100/REQ-105 narrowly refine legacy batch-fatal error routing for explicitly classified candidate-specific invalidity under REQ-104 and model-validity checks. They do not loosen validity checks themselves or REQ-048 hardware/REQ-096 Cycle terminal policies; invalid source data and program errors remain terminal. Worker infrastructure retries remain pending DISC-098.

DEC-101/REQ-106 reject the unaccepted one-retry proposal in DISC-098 and replace the inspected legacy startup/unexpected-exit retry behavior for the redesigned scheduler. Preserve REQ-048 memory termination, REQ-105 candidate-invalidity skip and REQ-096 Cycle-shortage stop. The user's requested hardware label is not treated as evidence establishing a hardware cause; accurate message wording remains pending DISC-099.
